The GEDI instrument consists of 3 lasers producing a total of 8 beam ground transects.
The eight remaining groups contain data for each of the eight GEDI beam transects.
For additional information, be sure to check out: https://gedi.umd.edu/instrument/specifications/.

The folder contains 28 GEDI granules with 8 BEAMS each. 

========= Beams'Type =========
BEAM0000 is a Coverage beam 
BEAM0001 is a Coverage beam 
BEAM0010 is a Coverage beam 
BEAM0011 is a Coverage beam 
BEAM0101 is a Full power beam 
BEAM0110 is a Full power beam 
BEAM1000 is a Full power beam 
BEAM1011 is a Full power beam
